# Day 01 — 2026-08-29

Zone 1 · 2,899 arc traversals · 6.37 h driving + 0.75 h break = **7.12 h**

Capture 2.05 h · deadhead 2.01 h · turns 2.31 h


Start and finish at the depot. **Capture** rows are required footage: keep to 40 km/h or below unless the row says *reduced*. **Deadhead** rows are repositioning — drive them normally, the camera is not counted.


> **36 arcs on this route are marked [UNVERIFIED].** They come from the customer's street layer and not from OpenStreetMap, so their direction, width and junction rules are assumptions, not tags. Treat them as two-way residential, obey what the signs actually say, and note any one-way, gate or building site you find — see `surveys/unverified-streets.md`.


> **295 streets on this route are marked [HOLD 40 — still owed].** You would otherwise have driven past them; filming them today is what saves a return trip later in the campaign. Hold to the capture speed on those rows. If you cannot — traffic, a closure, anything — say so, because the street then goes back on the list and the plan needs to know.
